# Source and terms log

Generated by `npm run terms-log` from `data/states/*.json`. Do not edit by hand.

Rules: sale dates come only from county or state government pages, or legally required notices, that we actually read. Private auction platforms and sources whose terms forbid reuse are link-only: we link to them and never copy their parcel data.

## Link-only sources

| Source | Why | Checked |
|---|---|---|
| [Maryland SDAT Real Property search](https://sdat.dat.maryland.gov/RealProperty) | Its terms forbid automated collection (scraping). We link to it and never copy from it. | 2026-10-26 |
| [Leon County, Florida tax deed portal](https://itm.leoncountyfl.gov/itm/CHMLogin.aspx) | Its license says the information may not be sold or distributed and is only for deed applications. Link only. | 2026-10-26 |
| [RealAuction sites (realtaxdeed.com, realtaxlien.com, realforeclose.com)](https://www.realauction.com/) | Private auction platform. No reuse terms found, so we link to county sale pages and never copy parcel data. | 2026-10-26 |
| [Bid4Assets](https://www.bid4assets.com/) | Private auction platform. No reuse terms found, so we link to county sale pages and never copy parcel data. | 2026-10-26 |
| [GovEase](https://www.govease.com/) | Private auction platform. Only privacy and cookie links found, so we link out and never copy parcel data. | 2026-10-26 |
| [West Virginia State Auditor's Office](https://www.wvsao.gov/CountyCollections/Default) | Its terms say electronic copies of data from the site are for individual use only and may not be resold or offered for payment without a contract. We link to it and never copy from it. | 2026-10-27 |

Hosts the validator refuses as a sale source: `realauction.com`, `realtaxdeed.com`, `realtaxlien.com`, `realforeclose.com`, `bid4assets.com`, `govease.com`, `zeusauction.com`, `sriservices.com`, `lienhub.com`, `grantstreet.com`, `govdeals.com`, `publicsurplus.com`, `wisconsinsurplus.com`, `marylandtaxsale.com`, `aarauctions.com`, `auctionsinternational.com`, `mnbid.mn.gov`, `newjerseytaxsale.com`, `sheriffsaleauction.ohio.gov`, `sheriffsaleauctions.com`, `coloradotaxsale.com`, `arizonataxsale.com`, `deedauction.net`, `clerkauction.com`, `itm.leoncountyfl.gov`, `sdat.dat.maryland.gov`, `mytaxsale.com`, `tacssale.com`, `vaauctionco.com`, `whiteoakhomesks.com`, `hibid.com`, `iowataxauction.com`, `wvsao.gov`.
